# Known Color Contrast Issues (Axe WCAG AA Audit)

> [!NOTE]
> All color tokens, shadow colors, opacities, and gradients are strictly locked under the theme governance policy (`docs/THEME_LOCK.md`). Accessibility warnings identified by Axe-core are tracked here as informational records rather than modified without explicit architectural authorization.

## Summary

- **Total Tracked Violations**: 4 distinct deduplicated element classes (down from 56 raw duplicate warnings)
- **Accent-Derived Contrast Issues**: **0** (100% resolved across all screens via `var(--on-accent)`)
- **Independent Status & Badge Contrast Issues**: **4** (strictly preserved per lock mandate)
- **Worst Ratio (Light Theme)**: `1.46:1` (Rating star `text-amber-400` on cold-press vellum `--bg-surface` `#edf1f3`)
- **Worst Ratio (Dark Theme)**: None (`> 7.5:1` across all accent surfaces)

---

## Section 1: Accent-Derived Surfaces (Resolved: 0 Remaining)

All accent-filled interactive surfaces (active tabs, primary CTA buttons, chapter play buttons, upload badges, and hero controls) have been transitioned from hardcoded light text (`text-white`) to dynamic `var(--on-accent)` tokens:

- **Dark Theme Accent**: `oklch(0.72 0.200 55)` (gamut-maps in sRGB to `#ff7900`)
  - **On-Accent Text**: `var(--on-accent)` resolves to `oklch(0.12 0.010 260)` (`#16181f`)
  - **Effective Contrast Ratio**: **7.73:1** (Exceeds WCAG AAA standard of 7.0:1)
  - **Remaining Violations**: **0**
- **Light Theme Accent**: `oklch(0.52 0.170 48)` (deep warm terracotta/amber)
  - **On-Accent Text**: `var(--on-accent)` resolves to `oklch(1.00 0.000 0)` (`#ffffff`)
  - **Effective Contrast Ratio**: **4.58:1** (Meets WCAG AA standard of 4.5:1)
  - **Remaining Violations**: **0**

---

## Section 2: Independent Status Badges & Presets (Locked & Preserved)

The following UI elements use independent status colors (`amber-400`, `emerald-400`) and editorial styling. By user architectural decree, these colors remain strictly locked and are not modified to satisfy algorithmic contrast checkers.

| Home & Catalog (Light) | `article .text-emerald-400.font-semibold` | Voice match score (`98% Match`) | `#34d399` | `#edf1f3` | 1.69:1 | 4.5:1 | Semantic emerald voice affinity indicator; locked per brand identity |
